Generated FRISC Assembly Code 
; Program entry point
        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
; Local variable a at (R5-04)
; Local variable b at (R5-08)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        SUB R7, %D 8, R7        ; allocate 2 local variables
; Compound statement
; Local variable declarations
; Local variable a at (R5-04) with initializer
        MOVE %D 98304, R0       ; load float constant 1.5 (Q16.16)
        STORE R0, (R5-04)       ; initialize a
; Local variable b at (R5-08) with initializer
        MOVE %D 131072, R0      ; load float constant 2.0 (Q16.16)
        STORE R0, (R5-08)       ; initialize b
        LOAD R0, (R5-04)        ; load variable a
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        LOAD R0, (R5-08)        ; load variable b
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FADD             ; call F_FADD
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; deallocate local variables
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Float helper functions (IEEE-754 single-precision)

F_FADD                          ; Helper function: float add(float a, float b)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (left operand, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (right operand, Q16.16)
        ADD R0, R1, R0          ; add Q16.16 values
        MOVE R0, R6             ; result (Q16.16)
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return

float_chained_mul.c

Lexer: OK Parser: OK Semantic: OK CodeGen: OK
Source Code 
// EXPECT: 13.125
// Q16.16: 860160 (13.125 * 65536 = 860160)

float main(void) {
    float x = 1.5;
    float y = 2.5;
    float z = 3.5;
    return x * y * z;
}

Generated FRISC Assembly Code 
; Program entry point
        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
; Local variable x at (R5-04)
; Local variable y at (R5-08)
; Local variable z at (R5-0C)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        SUB R7, %D 12, R7       ; allocate 3 local variables
; Compound statement
; Local variable declarations
; Local variable x at (R5-04) with initializer
        MOVE %D 98304, R0       ; load float constant 1.5 (Q16.16)
        STORE R0, (R5-04)       ; initialize x
; Local variable y at (R5-08) with initializer
        MOVE %D 163840, R0      ; load float constant 2.5 (Q16.16)
        STORE R0, (R5-08)       ; initialize y
; Local variable z at (R5-0C) with initializer
        MOVE %D 229376, R0      ; load float constant 3.5 (Q16.16)
        STORE R0, (R5-0C)       ; initialize z
        LOAD R0, (R5-04)        ; load variable x
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        LOAD R0, (R5-08)        ; load variable y
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FMUL             ; call F_FMUL
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        LOAD R0, (R5-0C)        ; load variable z
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FMUL             ; call F_FMUL
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        ADD R7, %D 12, R7       ; deallocate local variables
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Float helper functions (IEEE-754 single-precision)

F_FMUL                          ; Helper function: float mul(float a, float b)
; F_FMUL: Q16.16 fixed-point multiplication using 32-bit Russian peasant algorithm
; Q16.16 representable range: [-32768.0, +32767.9999]
; Safe multiplication domain: [-181.0, +181.0] per operand (no overflow)
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a * b (Q16.16 format)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (multiplicand, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (multiplier, Q16.16)
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if a == 0, return 0
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if b == 0, return 0
        MOVE %D 1, R4           ; sign = +1
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_SGE L4               ; a >= 0 → nothing
L5                              ; negate a
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0 (a_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L4                              ; a sign handled
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_SGE L6               ; b >= 0 → nothing
L7                              ; negate b
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R1, R1          ; R1 = -R1 (b_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L6                              ; b sign handled
; 64-bit Russian peasant multiplication: product = |a| * |b|
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign on stack
        MOVE %D 0, R3           ; M_HI = 0 (high 32 bits of multiplicand)
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; ACC_LO = 0
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; ACC_HI = 0
L8                              ; 64-bit multiplication loop
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L9                ; if B == 0, exit loop
        AND R1, %D 1, R6        ; R6 = B & 1
        CMP R6, %D 0
        JR_EQ L10               ; if (B & 1) == 0, skip add
        ADD R2, R0, R2          ; ACC_LO += M_LO
        ADC R4, R3, R4          ; ACC_HI += M_HI + carry
L10                             ; skip add
        MOVE R0, R6             ; save old M_LO
        SHL R0, %D 1, R0        ; M_LO <<= 1
        SHR R6, %D 31, R6       ; R6 = (old M_LO >> 31) & 1 (carry bit)
        SHL R3, %D 1, R3        ; M_HI <<= 1
        OR R3, R6, R3           ; M_HI |= carry_from_LO
        SHR R1, %D 1, R1        ; B >>= 1
        JR L8                   ; continue loop
L9                              ; multiplication done
; Q16.16 scaling: extract middle 32 bits from 64-bit product
        MOVE R2, R0             ; R0 = ACC_LO
        MOVE R4, R1             ; R1 = ACC_HI
        SHR R0, %D 16, R0       ; R0 = ACC_LO >> 16 (low 16 bits of result)
        SHL R1, %D 16, R1       ; R1 = ACC_HI << 16 (high 16 bits)
        OR R0, R1, R0           ; result_abs = (ACC_HI << 16) | (ACC_LO >> 16)
; Apply sign to result
        POP R4                  ; restore sign from stack
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JR_EQ L11               ; if sign == 0, result is 0
        JR_SLT L12              ; if sign < 0, negate
        JP L13                  ; sign > 0, no change
L11                             ; result is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R0           ; R0 = 0
        JP L13                  ; jump to done
L12                             ; negate result
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0
L13                             ; sign applied
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value in R6
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
        JP L3                   ; skip zero handler
L2                              ; zero operand
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; return 0 for zero operand
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
L3                              ; end of function

; Program entry point
        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
; Local variable a at (R5-04)
; Local variable b at (R5-08)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        SUB R7, %D 8, R7        ; allocate 2 local variables
; Compound statement
; Local variable declarations
; Local variable a at (R5-04) with initializer
        MOVE %D 327680, R0      ; load float constant 5.0 (Q16.16)
        STORE R0, (R5-04)       ; initialize a
; Local variable b at (R5-08) with initializer
        MOVE %D 131072, R0      ; load float constant 2.0 (Q16.16)
        STORE R0, (R5-08)       ; initialize b
        LOAD R0, (R5-04)        ; load variable a
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        LOAD R0, (R5-08)        ; load variable b
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FDIV             ; call F_FDIV
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; deallocate local variables
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Float helper functions (IEEE-754 single-precision)

F_FDIV                          ; Helper function: float div(float a, float b)
; F_FDIV: Q16.16 fixed-point division using 32-bit arithmetic
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a / b (Q16.16 format)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (dividend, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (divisor, Q16.16)
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if b == 0, return 0
        MOVE %D 1, R4           ; sign = +1
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_EQ L5                ; a == 0 → sign = 0
        JR_SLT L4               ; a < 0 → negate
        JP L3                   ; a > 0 → nothing
L5                              ; a is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; sign = 0 (result will be 0)
        JP L3
L4                              ; negate a
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0 (a_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L3                              ; a sign handled
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_SGE L6               ; b >= 0 → nothing
L7                              ; negate b
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R1, R1          ; R1 = -R1 (b_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L6                              ; b sign handled
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if sign == 0, result is 0
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign on stack
; 32-bit unsigned division: integer_part = a_abs / b_abs
        MOVE R0, R4             ; save dividend a_abs in R4
        MOVE %D 0, R0           ; integer_part = 0 (quotient)
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; remainder = 0
        MOVE %D 31, R3          ; i = 31 (loop counter)
L8                              ; 32-bit integer division loop
        CMP R3, %D 0
        JR_SLT L9               ; if i < 0, done
        SHL R2, %D 1, R2        ; remainder <<= 1
        MOVE R4, R5             ; temp = dividend
        SHR R5, R3, R5          ; temp = dividend >> i
        AND R5, %D 1, R5        ; temp = (dividend >> i) & 1
        OR R2, R5, R2           ; remainder |= (dividend >> i) & 1
        CMP R2, R1
        JR_SLT L10              ; if remainder < b_abs, skip subtraction
        SUB R2, R1, R2          ; remainder -= b_abs
        MOVE %D 1, R5
        SHL R5, R3, R5          ; R5 = 1 << i
        OR R0, R5, R0           ; integer_part |= (1 << i)
L10                             ; skip subtraction
        SUB R3, %D 1, R3        ; i--
        JR L8                   ; continue loop
L9                              ; integer division done
; 16-step fractional refinement loop
        MOVE %D 0, R3           ; frac = 0 (fractional accumulator)
        MOVE %D 0, R5           ; i = 0 (loop counter)
L11                             ; fractional refinement loop
        CMP R5, %D 16
        JR_SGE L12              ; if i >= 16, done
        SHL R2, %D 1, R2        ; remainder <<= 1
        SHL R3, %D 1, R3        ; frac <<= 1
        CMP R2, R1
        JR_SLT L13              ; if remainder < b_abs, skip subtraction
        SUB R2, R1, R2          ; remainder -= b_abs
        OR R3, %D 1, R3         ; frac |= 1
L13                             ; skip subtraction
        ADD R5, %D 1, R5        ; i++
        JR L11                  ; continue loop
L12                             ; fractional loop done
; Combine integer_part and frac into Q16.16 result
        SHL R0, %D 16, R0       ; integer_part <<= 16
        OR R0, R3, R0           ; result_abs = (integer_part << 16) | frac
; Apply sign to result
        POP R4                  ; restore sign from stack
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JR_EQ L14               ; sign == 0 → result 0
        JR_SLT L15              ; sign < 0 → negate
        JP L16                  ; sign > 0 → no change
L14                             ; sign zero
        MOVE %D 0, R0
        JP L16
L15                             ; negate result
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0
L16                             ; sign applied
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value in R6
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
L2                              ; division by zero or dividend is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; return 0 for division by zero or zero dividend
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return

// EXPECT: 12.0

Generated FRISC Assembly Code 
; Program entry point
        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
; Local variable a at (R5-04)
; Local variable b at (R5-08)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        SUB R7, %D 8, R7        ; allocate 2 local variables
; Compound statement
; Local variable declarations
; Local variable a at (R5-04) with initializer
        MOVE %D 196608, R0      ; load float constant 3.0 (Q16.16)
        STORE R0, (R5-04)       ; initialize a
; Local variable b at (R5-08) with initializer
        MOVE %D 262144, R0      ; load float constant 4.0 (Q16.16)
        STORE R0, (R5-08)       ; initialize b
        LOAD R0, (R5-04)        ; load variable a
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        LOAD R0, (R5-08)        ; load variable b
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FMUL             ; call F_FMUL
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; deallocate local variables
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Float helper functions (IEEE-754 single-precision)

F_FMUL                          ; Helper function: float mul(float a, float b)
; F_FMUL: Q16.16 fixed-point multiplication using 32-bit Russian peasant algorithm
; Q16.16 representable range: [-32768.0, +32767.9999]
; Safe multiplication domain: [-181.0, +181.0] per operand (no overflow)
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a * b (Q16.16 format)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (multiplicand, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (multiplier, Q16.16)
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if a == 0, return 0
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if b == 0, return 0
        MOVE %D 1, R4           ; sign = +1
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_SGE L4               ; a >= 0 → nothing
L5                              ; negate a
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0 (a_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L4                              ; a sign handled
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_SGE L6               ; b >= 0 → nothing
L7                              ; negate b
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R1, R1          ; R1 = -R1 (b_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L6                              ; b sign handled
; 64-bit Russian peasant multiplication: product = |a| * |b|
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign on stack
        MOVE %D 0, R3           ; M_HI = 0 (high 32 bits of multiplicand)
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; ACC_LO = 0
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; ACC_HI = 0
L8                              ; 64-bit multiplication loop
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L9                ; if B == 0, exit loop
        AND R1, %D 1, R6        ; R6 = B & 1
        CMP R6, %D 0
        JR_EQ L10               ; if (B & 1) == 0, skip add
        ADD R2, R0, R2          ; ACC_LO += M_LO
        ADC R4, R3, R4          ; ACC_HI += M_HI + carry
L10                             ; skip add
        MOVE R0, R6             ; save old M_LO
        SHL R0, %D 1, R0        ; M_LO <<= 1
        SHR R6, %D 31, R6       ; R6 = (old M_LO >> 31) & 1 (carry bit)
        SHL R3, %D 1, R3        ; M_HI <<= 1
        OR R3, R6, R3           ; M_HI |= carry_from_LO
        SHR R1, %D 1, R1        ; B >>= 1
        JR L8                   ; continue loop
L9                              ; multiplication done
; Q16.16 scaling: extract middle 32 bits from 64-bit product
        MOVE R2, R0             ; R0 = ACC_LO
        MOVE R4, R1             ; R1 = ACC_HI
        SHR R0, %D 16, R0       ; R0 = ACC_LO >> 16 (low 16 bits of result)
        SHL R1, %D 16, R1       ; R1 = ACC_HI << 16 (high 16 bits)
        OR R0, R1, R0           ; result_abs = (ACC_HI << 16) | (ACC_LO >> 16)
; Apply sign to result
        POP R4                  ; restore sign from stack
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JR_EQ L11               ; if sign == 0, result is 0
        JR_SLT L12              ; if sign < 0, negate
        JP L13                  ; sign > 0, no change
L11                             ; result is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R0           ; R0 = 0
        JP L13                  ; jump to done
L12                             ; negate result
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0
L13                             ; sign applied
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value in R6
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
        JP L3                   ; skip zero handler
L2                              ; zero operand
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; return 0 for zero operand
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
L3                              ; end of function

Generated FRISC Assembly Code 
; Program entry point
        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
; Compound statement
        MOVE %D 65536, R0       ; load float constant 1.0 (Q16.16)
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        MOVE %D 131072, R0      ; load float constant 2.0 (Q16.16)
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FADD             ; call F_FADD
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE %D 0, R1           ; zero for negation
        SUB R1, R0, R0          ; unary minus
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Float helper functions (IEEE-754 single-precision)

F_FADD                          ; Helper function: float add(float a, float b)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (left operand, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (right operand, Q16.16)
        ADD R0, R1, R0          ; add Q16.16 values
        MOVE R0, R6             ; result (Q16.16)
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return

        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
; Compound statement
        MOVE %D 196608, R0      ; load float constant 3.0 (Q16.16)
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        MOVE %D 262144, R0      ; load float constant 4.0 (Q16.16)
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FMUL             ; call F_FMUL
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE %D 0, R1           ; zero for negation
        SUB R1, R0, R0          ; unary minus
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Float helper functions (IEEE-754 single-precision)

F_FMUL                          ; Helper function: float mul(float a, float b)
; F_FMUL: Q16.16 fixed-point multiplication using 32-bit Russian peasant algorithm
; Q16.16 representable range: [-32768.0, +32767.9999]
; Safe multiplication domain: [-181.0, +181.0] per operand (no overflow)
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a * b (Q16.16 format)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (multiplicand, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (multiplier, Q16.16)
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if a == 0, return 0
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if b == 0, return 0
        MOVE %D 1, R4           ; sign = +1
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_SGE L4               ; a >= 0 → nothing
L5                              ; negate a
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0 (a_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L4                              ; a sign handled
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_SGE L6               ; b >= 0 → nothing
L7                              ; negate b
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R1, R1          ; R1 = -R1 (b_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L6                              ; b sign handled
; 64-bit Russian peasant multiplication: product = |a| * |b|
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign on stack
        MOVE %D 0, R3           ; M_HI = 0 (high 32 bits of multiplicand)
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; ACC_LO = 0
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; ACC_HI = 0
L8                              ; 64-bit multiplication loop
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L9                ; if B == 0, exit loop
        AND R1, %D 1, R6        ; R6 = B & 1
        CMP R6, %D 0
        JR_EQ L10               ; if (B & 1) == 0, skip add
        ADD R2, R0, R2          ; ACC_LO += M_LO
        ADC R4, R3, R4          ; ACC_HI += M_HI + carry
L10                             ; skip add
        MOVE R0, R6             ; save old M_LO
        SHL R0, %D 1, R0        ; M_LO <<= 1
        SHR R6, %D 31, R6       ; R6 = (old M_LO >> 31) & 1 (carry bit)
        SHL R3, %D 1, R3        ; M_HI <<= 1
        OR R3, R6, R3           ; M_HI |= carry_from_LO
        SHR R1, %D 1, R1        ; B >>= 1
        JR L8                   ; continue loop
L9                              ; multiplication done
; Q16.16 scaling: extract middle 32 bits from 64-bit product
        MOVE R2, R0             ; R0 = ACC_LO
        MOVE R4, R1             ; R1 = ACC_HI
        SHR R0, %D 16, R0       ; R0 = ACC_LO >> 16 (low 16 bits of result)
        SHL R1, %D 16, R1       ; R1 = ACC_HI << 16 (high 16 bits)
        OR R0, R1, R0           ; result_abs = (ACC_HI << 16) | (ACC_LO >> 16)
; Apply sign to result
        POP R4                  ; restore sign from stack
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JR_EQ L11               ; if sign == 0, result is 0
        JR_SLT L12              ; if sign < 0, negate
        JP L13                  ; sign > 0, no change
L11                             ; result is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R0           ; R0 = 0
        JP L13                  ; jump to done
L12                             ; negate result
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0
L13                             ; sign applied
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value in R6
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
        JP L3                   ; skip zero handler
L2                              ; zero operand
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; return 0 for zero operand
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
L3                              ; end of function

; Program entry point
        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
; Compound statement
        MOVE %D 196608, R0      ; load float constant 3.0 (Q16.16)
        MOVE %D 0, R1           ; zero for negation
        SUB R1, R0, R0          ; unary minus
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        MOVE %D 262144, R0      ; load float constant 4.0 (Q16.16)
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FMUL             ; call F_FMUL
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Float helper functions (IEEE-754 single-precision)

F_FMUL                          ; Helper function: float mul(float a, float b)
; F_FMUL: Q16.16 fixed-point multiplication using 32-bit Russian peasant algorithm
; Q16.16 representable range: [-32768.0, +32767.9999]
; Safe multiplication domain: [-181.0, +181.0] per operand (no overflow)
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a * b (Q16.16 format)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (multiplicand, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (multiplier, Q16.16)
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if a == 0, return 0
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if b == 0, return 0
        MOVE %D 1, R4           ; sign = +1
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_SGE L4               ; a >= 0 → nothing
L5                              ; negate a
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0 (a_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L4                              ; a sign handled
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_SGE L6               ; b >= 0 → nothing
L7                              ; negate b
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R1, R1          ; R1 = -R1 (b_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L6                              ; b sign handled
; 64-bit Russian peasant multiplication: product = |a| * |b|
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign on stack
        MOVE %D 0, R3           ; M_HI = 0 (high 32 bits of multiplicand)
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; ACC_LO = 0
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; ACC_HI = 0
L8                              ; 64-bit multiplication loop
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L9                ; if B == 0, exit loop
        AND R1, %D 1, R6        ; R6 = B & 1
        CMP R6, %D 0
        JR_EQ L10               ; if (B & 1) == 0, skip add
        ADD R2, R0, R2          ; ACC_LO += M_LO
        ADC R4, R3, R4          ; ACC_HI += M_HI + carry
L10                             ; skip add
        MOVE R0, R6             ; save old M_LO
        SHL R0, %D 1, R0        ; M_LO <<= 1
        SHR R6, %D 31, R6       ; R6 = (old M_LO >> 31) & 1 (carry bit)
        SHL R3, %D 1, R3        ; M_HI <<= 1
        OR R3, R6, R3           ; M_HI |= carry_from_LO
        SHR R1, %D 1, R1        ; B >>= 1
        JR L8                   ; continue loop
L9                              ; multiplication done
; Q16.16 scaling: extract middle 32 bits from 64-bit product
        MOVE R2, R0             ; R0 = ACC_LO
        MOVE R4, R1             ; R1 = ACC_HI
        SHR R0, %D 16, R0       ; R0 = ACC_LO >> 16 (low 16 bits of result)
        SHL R1, %D 16, R1       ; R1 = ACC_HI << 16 (high 16 bits)
        OR R0, R1, R0           ; result_abs = (ACC_HI << 16) | (ACC_LO >> 16)
; Apply sign to result
        POP R4                  ; restore sign from stack
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JR_EQ L11               ; if sign == 0, result is 0
        JR_SLT L12              ; if sign < 0, negate
        JP L13                  ; sign > 0, no change
L11                             ; result is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R0           ; R0 = 0
        JP L13                  ; jump to done
L12                             ; negate result
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0
L13                             ; sign applied
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value in R6
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
        JP L3                   ; skip zero handler
L2                              ; zero operand
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; return 0 for zero operand
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
L3                              ; end of function

        MOVE %D 65536, R0       ; load float constant 1.0 (Q16.16)

        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FMUL             ; call F_FMUL
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FADD             ; call F_FADD
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Float helper functions (IEEE-754 single-precision)

F_FADD                          ; Helper function: float add(float a, float b)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (left operand, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (right operand, Q16.16)
        ADD R0, R1, R0          ; add Q16.16 values
        MOVE R0, R6             ; result (Q16.16)
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return

F_FMUL                          ; Helper function: float mul(float a, float b)
; F_FMUL: Q16.16 fixed-point multiplication using 32-bit Russian peasant algorithm
; Q16.16 representable range: [-32768.0, +32767.9999]
; Safe multiplication domain: [-181.0, +181.0] per operand (no overflow)
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a * b (Q16.16 format)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (multiplicand, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (multiplier, Q16.16)
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if a == 0, return 0
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if b == 0, return 0
        MOVE %D 1, R4           ; sign = +1
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_SGE L4               ; a >= 0 → nothing
L5                              ; negate a
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0 (a_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L4                              ; a sign handled
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_SGE L6               ; b >= 0 → nothing
L7                              ; negate b
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R1, R1          ; R1 = -R1 (b_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L6                              ; b sign handled
; 64-bit Russian peasant multiplication: product = |a| * |b|
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign on stack
        MOVE %D 0, R3           ; M_HI = 0 (high 32 bits of multiplicand)
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; ACC_LO = 0
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; ACC_HI = 0
L8                              ; 64-bit multiplication loop
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L9                ; if B == 0, exit loop
        AND R1, %D 1, R6        ; R6 = B & 1
        CMP R6, %D 0
        JR_EQ L10               ; if (B & 1) == 0, skip add
        ADD R2, R0, R2          ; ACC_LO += M_LO
        ADC R4, R3, R4          ; ACC_HI += M_HI + carry
L10                             ; skip add
        MOVE R0, R6             ; save old M_LO
        SHL R0, %D 1, R0        ; M_LO <<= 1
        SHR R6, %D 31, R6       ; R6 = (old M_LO >> 31) & 1 (carry bit)
        SHL R3, %D 1, R3        ; M_HI <<= 1
        OR R3, R6, R3           ; M_HI |= carry_from_LO
        SHR R1, %D 1, R1        ; B >>= 1
        JR L8                   ; continue loop
L9                              ; multiplication done
; Q16.16 scaling: extract middle 32 bits from 64-bit product
        MOVE R2, R0             ; R0 = ACC_LO
        MOVE R4, R1             ; R1 = ACC_HI
        SHR R0, %D 16, R0       ; R0 = ACC_LO >> 16 (low 16 bits of result)
        SHL R1, %D 16, R1       ; R1 = ACC_HI << 16 (high 16 bits)
        OR R0, R1, R0           ; result_abs = (ACC_HI << 16) | (ACC_LO >> 16)
; Apply sign to result
        POP R4                  ; restore sign from stack
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JR_EQ L11               ; if sign == 0, result is 0
        JR_SLT L12              ; if sign < 0, negate
        JP L13                  ; sign > 0, no change
L11                             ; result is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R0           ; R0 = 0
        JP L13                  ; jump to done
L12                             ; negate result
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0
L13                             ; sign applied
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value in R6
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
        JP L3                   ; skip zero handler
L2                              ; zero operand
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; return 0 for zero operand
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
L3                              ; end of function

        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
; Compound statement
        MOVE %D 65536, R0       ; load float constant 1.0 (Q16.16)
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        MOVE %D 131072, R0      ; load float constant 2.0 (Q16.16)
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FADD             ; call F_FADD
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        MOVE %D 131072, R0      ; load float constant 2.0 (Q16.16)
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FMUL             ; call F_FMUL
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Float helper functions (IEEE-754 single-precision)

F_FADD                          ; Helper function: float add(float a, float b)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (left operand, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (right operand, Q16.16)
        ADD R0, R1, R0          ; add Q16.16 values
        MOVE R0, R6             ; result (Q16.16)
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return

F_FMUL                          ; Helper function: float mul(float a, float b)
; F_FMUL: Q16.16 fixed-point multiplication using 32-bit Russian peasant algorithm
; Q16.16 representable range: [-32768.0, +32767.9999]
; Safe multiplication domain: [-181.0, +181.0] per operand (no overflow)
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a * b (Q16.16 format)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (multiplicand, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (multiplier, Q16.16)
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if a == 0, return 0
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if b == 0, return 0

        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_SGE L4               ; a >= 0 → nothing
L5                              ; negate a
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0 (a_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L4                              ; a sign handled
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_SGE L6               ; b >= 0 → nothing
L7                              ; negate b
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R1, R1          ; R1 = -R1 (b_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L6                              ; b sign handled
; 64-bit Russian peasant multiplication: product = |a| * |b|
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign on stack
        MOVE %D 0, R3           ; M_HI = 0 (high 32 bits of multiplicand)
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; ACC_LO = 0
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; ACC_HI = 0
L8                              ; 64-bit multiplication loop
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L9                ; if B == 0, exit loop
        AND R1, %D 1, R6        ; R6 = B & 1
        CMP R6, %D 0
        JR_EQ L10               ; if (B & 1) == 0, skip add
        ADD R2, R0, R2          ; ACC_LO += M_LO
        ADC R4, R3, R4          ; ACC_HI += M_HI + carry
L10                             ; skip add
        MOVE R0, R6             ; save old M_LO
        SHL R0, %D 1, R0        ; M_LO <<= 1
        SHR R6, %D 31, R6       ; R6 = (old M_LO >> 31) & 1 (carry bit)
        SHL R3, %D 1, R3        ; M_HI <<= 1
        OR R3, R6, R3           ; M_HI |= carry_from_LO
        SHR R1, %D 1, R1        ; B >>= 1
        JR L8                   ; continue loop
L9                              ; multiplication done
; Q16.16 scaling: extract middle 32 bits from 64-bit product
        MOVE R2, R0             ; R0 = ACC_LO
        MOVE R4, R1             ; R1 = ACC_HI
        SHR R0, %D 16, R0       ; R0 = ACC_LO >> 16 (low 16 bits of result)
        SHL R1, %D 16, R1       ; R1 = ACC_HI << 16 (high 16 bits)
        OR R0, R1, R0           ; result_abs = (ACC_HI << 16) | (ACC_LO >> 16)
; Apply sign to result
        POP R4                  ; restore sign from stack
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JR_EQ L11               ; if sign == 0, result is 0
        JR_SLT L12              ; if sign < 0, negate

L11                             ; result is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R0           ; R0 = 0
        JP L13                  ; jump to done
L12                             ; negate result
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0

        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
        JP L3                   ; skip zero handler

; Function definitions

        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
; Compound statement
        MOVE %D 196608, R0      ; load float constant 3.0 (Q16.16)
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        MOVE %D 131072, R0      ; load float constant 2.0 (Q16.16)
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FSUB             ; call F_FSUB
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Float helper functions (IEEE-754 single-precision)

F_FSUB                          ; Helper function: float sub(float a, float b)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (left operand, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (right operand, Q16.16)
        SUB R0, R1, R0          ; subtract Q16.16 values
        MOVE R0, R6             ; result (Q16.16)
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return

        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
; Local variable a at (R5-04)
; Local variable b at (R5-08)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        SUB R7, %D 8, R7        ; allocate 2 local variables
; Compound statement
; Local variable declarations
; Local variable a at (R5-04) with initializer
        MOVE %D 98304, R0       ; load float constant 1.5 (Q16.16)
        STORE R0, (R5-04)       ; initialize a
; Local variable b at (R5-08) with initializer
        MOVE %D 131072, R0      ; load float constant 2.0 (Q16.16)
        STORE R0, (R5-08)       ; initialize b
        LOAD R0, (R5-04)        ; load variable a
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        LOAD R0, (R5-08)        ; load variable b
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FSUB             ; call F_FSUB
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; deallocate local variables
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Float helper functions (IEEE-754 single-precision)

F_FSUB                          ; Helper function: float sub(float a, float b)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (left operand, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (right operand, Q16.16)
        SUB R0, R1, R0          ; subtract Q16.16 values
        MOVE R0, R6             ; result (Q16.16)
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return

Generated FRISC Assembly Code 
; Program entry point
        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
; Local variable a at (R5-04)
; Local variable b at (R5-08)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        SUB R7, %D 8, R7        ; allocate 2 local variables
; Compound statement
; Local variable declarations
; Local variable a at (R5-04) with initializer
        MOVE %D 8, R0           ; load constant 8
        STORE R0, (R5-04)       ; initialize a
; Local variable b at (R5-08) with initializer
        MOVE %D 4, R0           ; load constant 4
        STORE R0, (R5-08)       ; initialize b
        LOAD R0, (R5-04)        ; load variable a
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        LOAD R0, (R5-08)        ; load variable b
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg (divisor)
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg (dividend)
        CALL F_DIV              ; call division helper
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; clean up arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; deallocate local variables
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Helper functions for multiplication and division

F_DIV                           ; Helper function: int div(int a, int b)
; F_DIV: signed 32-bit integer division (truncation towards zero)
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a / b
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (dividend, pushed last, first parameter)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (divisor, pushed first, second parameter)
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; division by zero
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; sign = 0 (positive)
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_SGE L4               ; if a >= 0, skip negation
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; a = -a
        XOR R4, %D 1, R4        ; sign ^= 1
L4                              ; skip negate a
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_SGE L5               ; if b >= 0, skip negation
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R1, R1          ; b = -b
        XOR R4, %D 1, R4        ; sign ^= 1
L5                              ; skip negate b
; Binary long division: shift-subtract algorithm, O(32) steps
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign flag on stack
        MOVE R0, R4             ; copy dividend to R4 (we'll need it in loop)
        MOVE %D 0, R0           ; q = 0 (quotient)
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; r = 0 (remainder)
        MOVE %D 31, R3          ; i = 31 (loop counter)
L6                              ; binary division loop (i = 31 down to 0)
        CMP R3, %D 0
        JR_SLT L7               ; if i < 0, done
        SHL R2, %D 1, R2        ; r <<= 1
        MOVE R4, R5             ; temp = dividend
        SHR R5, R3, R5          ; temp = dividend >> i
        AND R5, %D 1, R5        ; temp = (dividend >> i) & 1
        OR R2, R5, R2           ; r |= (dividend >> i) & 1
        CMP R2, R1
        JR_SLT L8               ; if r < b, skip subtraction
        SUB R2, R1, R2          ; r -= b
        MOVE %D 1, R5
        SHL R5, R3, R5          ; R5 = 1 << i
        OR R0, R5, R0           ; q |= (1 << i)
L8                              ; skip subtraction
        SUB R3, %D 1, R3        ; i--
        JR L6                   ; continue loop
L7                              ; loop done, apply sign
        POP R4                  ; restore sign flag
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JR_EQ L3                ; if sign == 0, result is positive
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; q = -q
        JP L3                   ; result ready
L2                              ; division by zero
        MOVE %D 0, R0           ; result 0 for division by zero
L3                              ; end division
        MOVE R0, R6             ; result
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

Generated FRISC Assembly Code 
; Program entry point
        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
; Local variable a at (R5-04)
; Local variable b at (R5-08)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        SUB R7, %D 8, R7        ; allocate 2 local variables
; Compound statement
; Local variable declarations
; Local variable a at (R5-04) with initializer
        MOVE %D 3, R0           ; load constant 3
        STORE R0, (R5-04)       ; initialize a
; Local variable b at (R5-08) with initializer
        MOVE %D 4, R0           ; load constant 4
        STORE R0, (R5-08)       ; initialize b
        LOAD R0, (R5-04)        ; load variable a
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        LOAD R0, (R5-08)        ; load variable b
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg (right operand)
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg (left operand)
        CALL F_MUL              ; call multiplication helper
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; clean up arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; deallocate local variables
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Helper functions for multiplication and division

F_MUL                           ; Helper function: int mul(int a, int b)
; F_MUL: signed 32-bit integer multiplication
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a * b
; Algorithm: binary shift-and-add (Russian peasant), O(32) steps
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (left operand, pushed last, first parameter)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (right operand, pushed first, second parameter)
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if b == 0, return 0
L3                              ; continue multiplication
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; sign = 0 (positive)
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_SGE L4               ; if a >= 0, skip negation
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; a = -a
        XOR R4, %D 1, R4        ; sign ^= 1
L4                              ; skip negate a
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_SGE L5               ; if b >= 0, skip negation
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R1, R1          ; b = -b
        XOR R4, %D 1, R4        ; sign ^= 1
L5                              ; skip negate b
; Binary shift-and-add multiplication (Russian peasant algorithm)
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign flag on stack
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; acc = 0 (accumulator)
L6                              ; binary multiplication loop
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L7                ; if b == 0, exit loop
        AND R1, %D 1, R3        ; R3 = b & 1
        CMP R3, %D 0
        JR_EQ L8                ; if (b & 1) == 0, skip add
        ADD R2, R0, R2          ; acc += a
L8                              ; skip add
        SHL R0, %D 1, R0        ; a <<= 1
        SHR R1, %D 1, R1        ; b >>= 1
        JR L6                   ; continue loop
L7                              ; loop done, apply sign
        POP R4                  ; restore sign flag
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JR_EQ L9                ; if sign == 0, result is positive
        MOVE %D 0, R0
        SUB R0, R2, R2          ; acc = -acc
        JP L9                   ; result ready
L2                              ; zero multiplier
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; result 0 for zero multiplier
L9                              ; end multiplication
        MOVE R2, R6             ; result
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Program entry point
        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
; Compound statement
        MOVE %D 3, R0           ; load constant 3
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        MOVE %D 4, R0           ; load constant 4
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg (right operand)
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg (left operand)
        CALL F_MUL              ; call multiplication helper
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; clean up arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE %D 0, R1           ; zero for negation
        SUB R1, R0, R0          ; unary minus
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Helper functions for multiplication and division

F_MUL                           ; Helper function: int mul(int a, int b)
; F_MUL: signed 32-bit integer multiplication
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a * b
; Algorithm: binary shift-and-add (Russian peasant), O(32) steps
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (left operand, pushed last, first parameter)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (right operand, pushed first, second parameter)
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if b == 0, return 0
L3                              ; continue multiplication
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; sign = 0 (positive)
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_SGE L4               ; if a >= 0, skip negation
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; a = -a
        XOR R4, %D 1, R4        ; sign ^= 1
L4                              ; skip negate a
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_SGE L5               ; if b >= 0, skip negation
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R1, R1          ; b = -b
        XOR R4, %D 1, R4        ; sign ^= 1
L5                              ; skip negate b
; Binary shift-and-add multiplication (Russian peasant algorithm)
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign flag on stack
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; acc = 0 (accumulator)
L6                              ; binary multiplication loop
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L7                ; if b == 0, exit loop
        AND R1, %D 1, R3        ; R3 = b & 1
        CMP R3, %D 0
        JR_EQ L8                ; if (b & 1) == 0, skip add
        ADD R2, R0, R2          ; acc += a
L8                              ; skip add
        SHL R0, %D 1, R0        ; a <<= 1
        SHR R1, %D 1, R1        ; b >>= 1
        JR L6                   ; continue loop
L7                              ; loop done, apply sign
        POP R4                  ; restore sign flag
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JR_EQ L9                ; if sign == 0, result is positive
        MOVE %D 0, R0
        SUB R0, R2, R2          ; acc = -acc
        JP L9                   ; result ready
L2                              ; zero multiplier
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; result 0 for zero multiplier
L9                              ; end multiplication
        MOVE R2, R6             ; result
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Output: R6 = a * b

; Program entry point
        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
; Compound statement
        MOVE %D 3, R0           ; load constant 3
        MOVE %D 0, R1           ; zero for negation
        SUB R1, R0, R0          ; unary minus
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        MOVE %D 4, R0           ; load constant 4
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg (right operand)
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg (left operand)
        CALL F_MUL              ; call multiplication helper
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; clean up arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Helper functions for multiplication and division

F_MUL                           ; Helper function: int mul(int a, int b)
; F_MUL: signed 32-bit integer multiplication
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a * b
; Algorithm: binary shift-and-add (Russian peasant), O(32) steps
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (left operand, pushed last, first parameter)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (right operand, pushed first, second parameter)
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if b == 0, return 0
L3                              ; continue multiplication
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; sign = 0 (positive)
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_SGE L4               ; if a >= 0, skip negation
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; a = -a
        XOR R4, %D 1, R4        ; sign ^= 1
L4                              ; skip negate a
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_SGE L5               ; if b >= 0, skip negation
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R1, R1          ; b = -b
        XOR R4, %D 1, R4        ; sign ^= 1
L5                              ; skip negate b
; Binary shift-and-add multiplication (Russian peasant algorithm)
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign flag on stack
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; acc = 0 (accumulator)
L6                              ; binary multiplication loop
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L7                ; if b == 0, exit loop
        AND R1, %D 1, R3        ; R3 = b & 1
        CMP R3, %D 0
        JR_EQ L8                ; if (b & 1) == 0, skip add
        ADD R2, R0, R2          ; acc += a
L8                              ; skip add
        SHL R0, %D 1, R0        ; a <<= 1
        SHR R1, %D 1, R1        ; b >>= 1
        JR L6                   ; continue loop
L7                              ; loop done, apply sign
        POP R4                  ; restore sign flag
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JR_EQ L9                ; if sign == 0, result is positive
        MOVE %D 0, R0
        SUB R0, R2, R2          ; acc = -acc
        JP L9                   ; result ready
L2                              ; zero multiplier
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; result 0 for zero multiplier
L9                              ; end multiplication
        MOVE R2, R6             ; result
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Program entry point
        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
; Local variable a at (R5-04)
; Local variable b at (R5-08)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        SUB R7, %D 8, R7        ; allocate 2 local variables
; Compound statement
; Local variable declarations
; Local variable a at (R5-04) with initializer
        MOVE %D 3, R0           ; load constant 3
        STORE R0, (R5-04)       ; initialize a
; Local variable b at (R5-08) with initializer
        MOVE %D 4, R0           ; load constant 4
        STORE R0, (R5-08)       ; initialize b
        LOAD R0, (R5-04)        ; load variable a
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        LOAD R0, (R5-08)        ; load variable b
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        SUB R0, R1, R0          ; subtraction
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; deallocate local variables
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller
